- **deskless**: Sets up a personal, always-on Claude Code server ("box") on Hetzner that the person uses from the Claude phone app or claude.ai/code through Remote Control, reachable only over their own Tailscale network. Also lists their boxes and deletes one. Walks a beginner through every step, Hetzner and Tailscale accounts included. Use when someone wants Claude Code in the cloud, on a server or VPS, running while their laptop is off, or on their phone; or wants to see or delete a deskless box.
